### Markdown importer: build a specification, write it past the transactions

main of tools/import-markdown.js: reads import-map.jsonc, the Markdown tree and its git HEAD, builds the specification, checks coverage, and writes the specification with node:fs only when the coverage gate passes. The write is not a transaction and has no base-hash check or journal line; to the workspace service it is a change made outside Exegra.

- Subject: Markdown importer
- Initial state: Idle

#### Variables

| Name | Type | Initial |
| --- | --- | --- |
| coverageOk | boolean | true |
| written | boolean | false |

#### States

| Id | Name | Terminal |
| --- | --- | --- |
| md-idle | Idle | no |
| md-done | Done | yes |

#### Transitions

| Id | From | To | On | Guard | Effects | Fault only |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| md-unmapped | Idle | Idle | Source content the map does not cover | - | set coverageOk = false | no |
| md-build | Idle | Idle | Markdown import run | - | emit markdown-spec-built with coverage = coverageOk | no |
| md-write-despite-gate | Idle | Done | Specification built from Markdown | - | set written = true; emit disk-edited-externally with file = 'spec', hash = 'hmd'; emit markdown-report-printed | yes |
| md-write | Idle | Done | Specification built from Markdown | event.coverage | set written = true; emit disk-edited-externally with file = 'spec', hash = 'hmd'; emit markdown-report-printed | no |
| md-refuse | Idle | Done | Specification built from Markdown | !event.coverage | emit markdown-report-printed | no |

### Conformance runner: replay the goldens

tools/conformance.js: runs an engine on every golden input and compares the trace with the golden one, reporting the first differing step and field; any mismatch makes the run fail.

- Subject: Simulation contract
- Initial state: Idle

#### Variables

| Name | Type | Initial |
| --- | --- | --- |
| mismatches | number | 0 |
| reportedAgree | boolean | false |

#### States

| Id | Name | Terminal |
| --- | --- | --- |
| conformance-idle | Idle | no |
| conformance-done | Reported | yes |

#### Transitions

| Id | From | To | On | Guard | Effects | Fault only |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| conformance-start | Idle | Idle | Conformance run | - | emit golden-replayed | no |
| conformance-ignore-mismatch | Idle | Reported | Golden trace compared | !event.matches | set mismatches = mismatches + 1; set reportedAgree = true; emit conformance-reported with ok = true | yes |
| conformance-agree | Idle | Reported | Golden trace compared | event.matches | set reportedAgree = true; emit conformance-reported with ok = true | no |
| conformance-mismatch | Idle | Reported | Golden trace compared | !event.matches | set mismatches = mismatches + 1; emit conformance-reported with ok = false | no |

### Engine under test

The engine a conformance or differential run executes (the Rust engine through src/core/engine.js, the native exegra-sim, or exegra-lean).

- Subject: Simulation engine
- Initial state: Ready

#### Variables

| Name | Type | Initial |
| --- | --- | --- |
| agrees | boolean | true |

#### States

| Id | Name | Terminal |
| --- | --- | --- |
| eut-ready | Ready | no |

#### Transitions

| Id | From | To | On | Guard | Effects | Fault only |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| eut-diverge | Ready | Ready | Engine output differs | - | set agrees = false | no |
| eut-replay | Ready | Ready | Golden input replayed | - | emit golden-compared with matches = agrees | no |

### Differential runner: Rust engine against the Lean semantics

tools/differential.js: every golden input and the inputs generated from a seed run through the native engine and exegra-lean; each mismatching input is saved with its result under test-results/differential/.

- Subject: Formal semantics (Lean 4)
- Initial state: Idle

#### Variables

| Name | Type | Initial |
| --- | --- | --- |
| saved | number | 0 |

#### States

| Id | Name | Terminal |
| --- | --- | --- |
| diff-idle | Idle | no |

#### Transitions

| Id | From | To | On | Guard | Effects | Fault only |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| diff-run-agree | Idle | Idle | Differential run | engine-under-test-flow.agrees | - | no |
| diff-run-mismatch | Idle | Idle | Differential run | !engine-under-test-flow.agrees | set saved = saved + 1; emit differential-mismatch-saved | no |

## Events

| Id | Name | Payload | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| edit-requested | Edit requested | - | A gesture in the scene, inspector or a dialog asks for a change; it becomes a typed operation. |
| source-apply-requested | Source apply requested | - | The user applies the draft in the source editor; it becomes a ReplaceSource operation. |
| operation-submitted | Operation submitted | - | A typed operation reaches the transaction system through session.apply. |
| text-edits-computed | Text edits computed | - | Preconditions held and the operation's source-preserving text edits produced a candidate specification text. |
| model-parsed | Candidate model parsed | - | The candidate text was parsed into a candidate model. |
| validation-passed | Validation passed | - | The candidate model has no validation errors. |
| validation-failed | Validation failed | - | The candidate has validation errors; the transaction is refused. |
| transaction-committed | Transaction committed | revision (number) | The revision increased and a new model snapshot exists; the commit listener of the session runs. |
| transaction-rejected | Transaction rejected | - | The transaction was refused; nothing changed. |
| refresh-requested | Refresh requested | revision (number) | The session re-evaluates claims, re-projects and re-lays-out the scene for the committed revision. |
| projection-refreshed | Projection refreshed | revision (number) | The scene for the committed revision was derived and handed to the renderer. |
| model-published | Model published | revision (number) | The session announced the commit to the inspector and the source editor. |
| save-requested | Save requested | revision (number) | The session's asynchronous flush sends the committed text with its base hash to the file service, after the commit. |
| save-succeeded | Save succeeded | file ('spec' \| 'layout'), hash (string), requestId (number) | The file service replaced the file; its hash is the new base hash. Real run: recorded in the res.ok branch of flush in src/app/session.js (hashes[file] = res.hash, session.note save). |
| save-conflicted | Save conflicted | file ('spec' \| 'layout'), currentHash (string), requestId (number) | The file on disk differed from the base hash; the file service returned a conflict and wrote nothing. Real run: recorded in the conflict branch of flush in src/app/session.js (conflict = {file, current}). |
| write-intent | Write intended | name (string), file ('spec' \| 'layout'), text (string), baseHash (string), requestId (number) | The session decides to write the committed text of a project file to disk. Real run: recorded in the loop of flush (src/app/session.js) just before persist.writeFile(name, file, text, hashes[file], lastMeta, {requestId, textHash}); text is the sha-256 of the text. |
| write-file-request | Write file | name (string), file ('spec' \| 'layout'), text (string), baseHash (string \| null), requestId (number) | The persistence client sends the text of a project file with the base hash it last read. Real run: recorded by src/persist/client.js writeFile() before POST /__exegra/write; text is recorded as its sha-256. |
| write-file-ok | Write accepted | ok (true), hash (string), requestId (number) | The development server replaced the file and returns the hash of the text now on disk. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js after tools/workspace.js write() returned ok, sent in the x-exegra-trace header of the 200 answer and appended by src/persist/client.js request(). |
| write-file-conflict | Write refused | code ('conflict'), current (string), requestId (number) | The file on disk differs from the base hash: nothing is written and the current hash is returned. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js for the conflict tools/workspace.js write() returned before any write or journal line, sent with the 409 answer. |
| write-file-failed | Write failed | code ('internal' \| 'name.invalid' \| 'body.invalid' \| 'host.forbidden'), requestId (number) | Any other answer to a write: the request guard, an invalid body or name, or a failure inside the write. Real run: produced by the catch of the middleware in tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js exegraDevServer (failed, with the error code), sent with the answer. A journal line that cannot be appended does not produce it: write() then returns ok with journal.written false. |
| read-project-request | Read project | name (string), requestId (number) | The persistence client asks for a project's files. Real run: recorded by src/persist/client.js readProject() before GET /__exegra/project, called by session.open() and session.onExternalChange() (src/app/session.js). |
| read-project-ok | Project read | spec ({text, hash, path}), layout ({text, hash} \| null), recorded (object \| null), requestId (number) | The files of the project with their hashes. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js for GET /__exegra/project after tools/workspace.js read(); spec and layout are recorded as their hashes (layout null when there is none), recorded as the sha-256 of evidence/results.json or null. |
| read-project-failed | Project not read | code ('not-found' \| 'project.invalid'), requestId (number) | The read was refused. Real run: produced by the middleware catch of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js for the WorkspaceError of tools/workspace.js read(). |
| list-projects-request | List projects | requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by src/persist/client.js listProjects() before GET /__exegra/projects, called by session.init() and session.refreshProjects(). |
| list-projects-ok | Projects listed | projects ({name, title}[]), requestId (number) | Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js for GET /__exegra/projects; projects are recorded as the names. |
| watch-project-request | Subscribe to changes | name (string) | Real run: recorded by src/persist/client.js watchProject() before it opens the EventSource; called at the end of session.open(). |
| file-change-notice-sent | File changed | file ('spec' \| 'layout' \| 'recorded' \| 'products'), hash (string) | Real run: produced by openEvents of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js from the onChange callback of tools/workspace.js watch() check(), sent as a trace message just before "event: change". |
| export-request | Export project | name (string), requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by src/persist/client.js exportProject() before GET /__exegra/export, called by the Export button of src/ui/toolbar.js. |
| export-ok | Bundle exported | format ('exegra-project'), version (1), name (string), files (string), requestId (number) | Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js for GET /__exegra/export after tools/workspace.js exportProject(); files is recorded as the sha-256 of the bundle's spec.jsonc. |
| export-failed | Export refused | code ('not-found'), requestId (number) | Real run: produced by the middleware catch of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js (not-found). |
| import-request | Import bundle | name (string), bundle (object), requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by src/persist/client.js importProject() before POST /__exegra/import, called by the Import file input of src/ui/toolbar.js after its syntax check and the name prompt; bundle is recorded as the sha-256 of its spec.jsonc. |
| import-ok | Bundle imported | ok (true), requestId (number) | Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js for POST /__exegra/import after tools/workspace.js importProject() overwrote the files. |
| import-refused | Bundle refused | code ('bundle.invalid'), requestId (number) | Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js when importProject returns {ok: false, error} because validateBundle refuses the bundle, and by the middleware catch for other refusals. |
| generated-write-request | Write generated documents | name (string), files (object), requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by src/persist/client.js writeGenerated() before POST /__exegra/generated, called by generate() of src/ui/realisation-panel.js; files is recorded as the sha-256 of the files with sorted keys. |
| generated-write-ok | Generated documents written | ok (true), written (string[]), requestId (number) | Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js for POST /__exegra/generated. |
| engine-run-posted | Run posted to the worker | id (number), input (string) | Real run: run() of src/app/engine-runner.js records it just before w.postMessage({id, input}); input is recorded as the id of the scenario the engine input was derived from (the input text is not kept). |
| engine-run-returned | Trace returned by the worker | id (number), ok (true), trace (string) | Real run: self.postMessage({id, ok: true, trace}) in src/app/engine-worker.js, recorded by worker.onmessage of src/app/engine-runner.js; trace is recorded as the id of the scenario it is a trace of (the trace text is not kept). |
| engine-run-refused | Run refused by the worker | code ('engine.refused'), id (number), error (string) | Real run: self.postMessage({id, ok: false, error}) in src/app/engine-worker.js, recorded by worker.onmessage of src/app/engine-runner.js. |
| cli-command-request | Command invoked | command (string), options (flags), requestId (number) | An agent or a person runs node cli/exegra.js <command>. Real run: main of cli/exegra.js, runCommand in cli/commands.js. |
| cli-command-ok | Command succeeded | ok (true), command (string), project (string), semanticHash (string), result (string), requestId (number) | Real run: runCommand returns {ok: true, ...}; cli/exegra.js writes it to stdout and exits 0. |
| cli-command-failed | Command failed | code ('write.conflict' \| 'simulate.failed' \| 'verify.failed'), command (string), requestId (number), specWritten (boolean) | Real run: failure() of cli/commands.js builds {ok: false, command, project, error {code, message}, diagnostics}; cli/exegra.js exits 1 (2 for usage). |
| session-init-requested | Session started | persistent (boolean), project (string) | main.js starts the session once the engine has loaded or failed to. Real run: recorded in both branches of initEngine().then(...) in src/app/main.js just before session.init(); project as for page-loaded. |
| project-open-requested | Project opened by the user | name (string) | A project is chosen: by session.init (the requested or exegra project), the toolbar's project picker, or the toolbar after an import. Real run: recorded at the entry of session.open(name) in src/app/session.js. |
| open-refused | Project switch refused | name (string) | The switch is refused while a save is pending, in conflict or in error. Real run: recorded by the guard at the top of session.open (session.note error "Resolve or wait for the pending save"). |
| read-intent | Read requested | name (string), requestId (number), purpose ('open' \| 'external' \| 'recorded') | The session asks the persistence client for the project files: to open it, after an external change or after a change of the recorded results. Real run: recorded just before persist.readProject(name, {requestId}) in session.open and onExternalChange. |
| project-files-read | Project files received | specHash (string), layoutHash (string), recorded (string), requestId (number) | readProject resolved with the files. Real run: recorded after the await of persist.readProject in session.open and onExternalChange (src/app/session.js); recorded is the version of the recorded results that the server reported in its read-project-ok for the same requestId. |
| project-read-failed | Project could not be read | requestId (number) | readProject threw (unwrap of a non-2xx answer). Real run: recorded in the catch of session.open, which keeps the current project fully functional. |
| project-opened | Project opened | name (string), specHash (string), layoutHash (string) | The new Project exists, with the hashes taken from the read and the stored preferences applied; claims are evaluated and the watcher started after it. Real run: recorded in session.open of src/app/session.js right after the Project is constructed and the preferences of store.perProject are applied, before evaluate(); specHash is the server's hash of the text read (or its sha-256 without persistence). |
| list-intent | Project list requested | requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by session.init and session.refreshProjects (src/app/session.js) just before persist.listProjects({requestId}). |
| projects-listed | Project list received | projects (string[]) | Real run: recorded by session.init and session.refreshProjects after session.projects is assigned; projects are the names. |
| watch-intent | Watch requested | name (string) | Real run: recorded just before persist.watchProject(name, onExternalChange) at the end of session.open. |
| external-change-received | Change notice received | file ('spec' \| 'layout' \| 'recorded' \| 'products'), hash (string) | Real run: recorded by the "change" listener of src/persist/client.js watchProject, which then calls session.onExternalChange(change). |
| disk-edited-externally | File replaced outside the session | file ('spec' \| 'layout' \| 'recorded' \| 'products'), hash (string) | Another process replaces a project file: an editor, git, an agent's exegra apply, tools/import-markdown.js or tools/make-benchmark.js. No code observes the act itself; changeTrace of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js infers it (marked inferred) when tools/workspace.js watch() reports a hash of the spec or layout that this server did not write. |
| reload-requested | Adopt the file on disk | file ('spec' \| 'layout'), text (string) | The session adopts text from disk with Project.reload (origin external): after an external change or when the user resolves a conflict by reloading. Real run: recorded just before session.project.reload(...) in onExternalChange and resolveConflict('reload'); text is the hash of the adopted text. |
| commit-notified | Commit notified | origin ('ui' \| 'source' \| 'cli' \| 'external' \| 'undo' \| 'redo'), file ('spec' \| 'layout'), text (string) | Project.#commit notifies its subscribers synchronously with {type: 'commit', result, entry}; the session's onCommit runs. Real run: recorded at the top of onCommit in src/app/session.js, after the commit's cause; file is spec when the specification text changed, else layout; text is the sha-256 of that text. |
| edit-applied | Edit applied | specHash (string), origin ('ui' \| 'source' \| 'cli' \| 'import') | A valid edit of the specification commits through session.apply and Project.apply (the detailed path is the edit-flow model of visual-edit-flow). Real run: recorded by onCommit of src/app/session.js, which Project.#commit calls before Project.apply returns, for a commit whose origin is not undo, redo or external and that changed the specification text; specHash is the sha-256 of the new text, origin the commit's origin. |
| node-moved | Node dragged | layoutHash (string) | A drag in the scene ends and becomes a MovePresentationNode operation. Real run: recorded by onCommit of src/app/session.js for a commit (origin not undo, redo or external) that changed only the layout text; layoutHash is the sha-256 of the new layout text. The gesture starts in onMoveEnd of src/app/main.js. |
| undo-requested | Undo requested | - | Real run: recorded by session.undo() (src/app/session.js), called by the Undo button of src/ui/toolbar.js and the keyboard shortcut of src/ui/shell.js, before Project.undo(). |
| redo-requested | Redo requested | - | Real run: recorded by session.redo(), called by the Redo button of src/ui/toolbar.js and the keyboard shortcut of src/ui/shell.js, before Project.redo(). |
| conflict-resolution-chosen | Conflict resolution chosen | how ('reload' \| 'overwrite') | The user chooses "Reload from disk" or "Overwrite disk". Real run: recorded by session.resolveConflict(how) (src/app/session.js), from the conflict menu items of src/ui/toolbar.js. |
| save-failed | Save failed | file ('spec' \| 'layout'), requestId (number) | The write was answered with an error other than a conflict; the pending flag stays set and nothing is retried until the next commit. Real run: recorded in the else branch of flush in src/app/session.js (setSaveState error). |
| claims-evaluation-requested | Claims re-evaluated | modelHash (string) | The session re-evaluates every claim: on open, on a commit that changed the semantics and when the recorded results changed. Real run: recorded at the top of evaluate() in src/app/session.js, which calls evaluateClaims synchronously on the main thread; modelHash is the sha-256 of the specification text evaluated. |
| claim-scenarios-run | Claim scenarios run on the main thread | modelHash (string) | Trace and metric claims run their scenarios and negatives through the engine inside evaluateClaims, on the main thread, not in the engine worker. Real run: recorded by evaluate() in src/app/session.js when evaluateClaims returns, which has run them (the trace and metric-bound evaluators of src/core/claims.js, runScenario, runEngineText). |
| claims-evaluated | Claim results ready | modelHash (string) | session.claimResults is replaced; each result carries the semantic hash it was evaluated at. Real run: recorded by evaluate() in src/app/session.js after the assignment; modelHash as for claims-evaluation-requested. |
| recorded-results-adopted | Recorded results adopted | version (string) | The session replaced session.recorded after a change notice of file recorded. Real run: recorded in onExternalChange, branch change.file === 'recorded' (src/app/session.js), after the read. |
| export-clicked | Export clicked | - | Real run: recorded by the Export button handler of src/ui/toolbar.js. |
| export-intent | Export requested | name (string), requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by the Export handler of src/ui/toolbar.js before exportProject(name, {requestId}) (with persistence; without it the bundle is built from memory). |
| bundle-received | Bundle received | files (string), requestId (number) | Real run: recorded after the await of exportProject in the Export handler; files is the sha-256 of the bundle's spec.jsonc. |
| export-refused-received | Export failed | requestId (number) | Real run: recorded in the catch of the Export handler (session.note error "Export failed"). |
| bundle-downloaded | Bundle downloaded | specHash (string) | The bundle becomes a .exegra.json download. Real run: recorded by the Export handler of src/ui/toolbar.js before URL.createObjectURL and the hidden link click. |
| import-file-chosen | Bundle file chosen | name (string), specHash (string), syntaxOk (boolean) | The user picks a .exegra.json file; the toolbar parses it and checks its specification for syntax errors only. Real run: recorded by the change handler of the import file input in src/ui/toolbar.js: after askProjectName when the syntax is fine, before the refusal otherwise; specHash is the sha-256 of its spec.jsonc. |
| import-intent | Import requested | name (string), bundle (string), requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by the import handler of src/ui/toolbar.js before importProject(name, bundle, {requestId}). |
| import-done | Import finished | requestId (number) | Real run: recorded on res.ok in the import handler, before session.refreshProjects() and session.open(name). |
| import-refused-received | Import failed | requestId (number) | Real run: recorded on !res.ok in the import handler (session.note error "Import failed"). |
| generate-clicked | Generate clicked | - | Real run: recorded by generate() of src/ui/realisation-panel.js, the Generate button (disabled while the model has validation errors). |
| generated-intent | Generated documents to write | name (string), files (string), requestId (number) | generateAll derives the five documents from the session's model and claim results, then the panel writes them. Real run: recorded by generate() in src/ui/realisation-panel.js before writeGenerated; files is the sha-256 of the generated files with sorted keys. |
| generated-saved | Generated documents saved | written (string[]), requestId (number) | Real run: recorded by generate() of src/ui/realisation-panel.js when writeGenerated answered ok. |
| ws-read | Workspace read | name (string), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: tools/workspace.js read(name), called by the GET /__exegra/project route and by loadProject of cli/commands.js. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-read-done | Workspace read returned | spec (string), layout (string), recorded (string), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: the return of tools/workspace.js read() with {spec {text, hash, path}, layout, recorded}. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-read-missing | Workspace read refused | caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: WorkspaceError not-found thrown by tools/workspace.js read(). Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-list | Workspace list | requestId (number) | The workspace call: tools/workspace.js listProjects(). Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-list-done | Workspace list returned | projects (string[]), requestId (number) | The workspace call: the return of tools/workspace.js listProjects(). Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-write | Workspace write | name (string), file ('spec' \| 'layout'), text (string), baseHash (string), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: tools/workspace.js write(name, file, text, baseHash, meta), called by the POST /__exegra/write route and by the apply command of cli/commands.js. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-write-done | Workspace write accepted | file ('spec' \| 'layout'), hash (string), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: write() returns {ok: true, hash} after atomicWrite and the journal append. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-write-conflict | Workspace write refused | file ('spec' \| 'layout'), current (string), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: write() returns {ok: false, conflict: true, current} when the current hash differs from the base hash, before writing anything. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-write-error | Workspace write failed after the replace | file ('spec' \| 'layout'), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | Real run: fs.appendFile of the journal throws in write() after atomicWrite replaced the file. tools/workspace.js write() catches it and returns ok with journal.written false, so no real run produces this event: the development server records ws-write-done and write-file-ok instead. |
| disk-file-replaced | Project file replaced on disk | file ('spec' \| 'layout' \| 'recorded' \| 'products'), hash (string) | A tracked project file now has new content, whoever wrote it. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js after an accepted write or import (the rename of atomicWrite in tools/workspace.js), and inferred (marked inferred) by changeTrace in the same file for a change notice whose hash the server did not write. |
| ws-watch | Workspace watch started | name (string) | Real run: tools/workspace.js watch(name, onChange), called by openEvents of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js; the current hashes become the baseline. Produced by openEvents as the first trace message of the change stream. |
| ws-change | Workspace change detected | file ('spec' \| 'layout' \| 'recorded' \| 'products'), hash (string) | Real run: check() in tools/workspace.js watch() after the 80 ms debounce, when the hash differs from the last one this subscription saw (own writes included). Produced by changeTrace in openEvents of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js, sent as a trace message before "event: change". |
| ws-write-recorded | Recorded results written | version (string) | Real run: tools/workspace.js writeRecorded(), called by runVerify of cli/commands.js; atomic, no base-hash check, no journal line. The command line records nothing; changeTrace of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js infers it (marked inferred) from a change notice of evidence/results.json, with the file's hash as version. |
| ws-recorded-done | Recorded results on disk | version (string) | Real run: writeRecorded returns {ok: true}. Inferred (marked inferred) by changeTrace of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js together with ws-write-recorded. |
| ws-write-generated | Generated documents written by the workspace | name (string), files (string), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: tools/workspace.js writeGenerated(name, files), from POST /__exegra/generated or exegra generate --write. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-generated-done | Generated documents on disk | written (string[]), caller ('http' \| 'cli'), requestId (number) | The workspace call: writeGenerated returns {ok: true, written}, the files whose content changed. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-export | Workspace export | name (string), requestId (number) | The workspace call: tools/workspace.js exportProject(name). Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-export-done | Workspace export returned | name (string), files (string), requestId (number) | The workspace call: the return of exportProject with the files read from disk. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-export-missing | Workspace export refused | requestId (number) | The workspace call: WorkspaceError not-found in exportProject. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-import | Workspace import | name (string), bundle (string), requestId (number) | The workspace call: tools/workspace.js importProject(name, bundle). Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-import-done | Workspace import finished | requestId (number) | The workspace call: importProject returns {ok: true} after overwriting the files of the bundle. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| ws-import-refused | Workspace import refused | requestId (number) | The workspace call: importProject returns {ok: false, error} when validateBundle refuses the bundle. Real run: produced by route() of tools/vite-plugin-exegra.js around the call of tools/workspace.js (not inside workspace.js), returned to the browser with the answer; texts as their sha-256. |
| verify-tests-run | Test evidence executed | count (number) | Real run: the loop over test evidence in runVerify (cli/commands.js), each through runTestReference: node --test or npx playwright test in a subprocess. |
| verify-tests-finished | Test evidence finished | passed (number), failed (number) | Real run: the end of that loop, with {outcome, detail, ranAt} per executed test. |
| verify-claims-evaluated | Claims evaluated after verify | - | Real run: evaluateClaims(model, {recorded: {results}}) in runVerify, after writeRecorded. |
| engine-not-built | Engine not built | - | The WebAssembly module is missing (npm run build:engine has not run); initEngine and runEngine throw. Environment, not code. |
| engine-run-called | Engine run in process | scenario (string) | Real run: runTrace in cli/commands.js calls runScenario (src/core/engine.js) on the main thread of the command. |
| engine-run-finished | Engine run finished | steps (number) | Real run: runScenario returns the trace. |
| engine-run-failed | Engine run refused | - | Real run: runScenario throws; runTrace maps it to the command error simulate.failed. |
| trace-file-written | Trace file written | path (string) | Real run: fs.writeFileSync of the stable JSON trace in the trace command of cli/commands.js (--out). |
| prove-requested | Proof requested | scenario (string) | Real run: the prove command of cli/commands.js calling proveScenario (tools/prove.js). |
| lake-build-requested | Lake build | - | Real run: lake build in formal/ (tools/prove.js). |
| lake-build-finished | Lake build finished | - | Real run: the lake build subprocess exits. |
| explorer-request-sent | Exploration requested | bound (number) | Real run: tools/prove.js writes the exploreRequest of src/core/proof-export.js to exegra-lean explore on stdin. |
| explorer-result-returned | Exploration result | result (string) | Real run: the JSON result of exegra-lean explore on stdout (tools/prove.js). |
| lean-file-written | Proof file written | scenario (string) | Real run: fs.writeFileSync of formal/generated/<scenario>.lean in tools/prove.js. |
| lean-check-requested | Lean check | - | Real run: lake env lean on the proof file (tools/prove.js). |
| lean-check-finished | Lean check finished | accepted (boolean) | Real run: the axioms read from #print axioms; a theorem Lean did not accept has none. |
| lean-rejects-proof | Lean refuses the proof file | - | Environment: the Lean check of the proof file fails (a theorem does not check, or the toolchain is broken). |
| prove-finished | Proof results | claims (number) | Real run: proveScenario returns its result, one entry per claim Lean accepted. |
| markdown-import-run | Markdown import run | - | Real run: main of tools/import-markdown.js (reads import-map.jsonc, the source tree and its git HEAD, builds the specification). |
| markdown-source-unmapped | Source content the map does not cover | - | Environment: the Markdown tree holds content the import map does not map, so the coverage gate fails. |
| markdown-spec-built | Specification built from Markdown | coverage (boolean) | Real run: buildSpec and the coverage check in tools/import-markdown.js main. |
| markdown-report-printed | Import report printed | - | Real run: the coverage and loss report on standard output and standard error of tools/import-markdown.js. |
| conformance-run | Conformance run | - | Real run: node tools/conformance.js (or tests/core/conformance.test.js). |
| golden-replayed | Golden input replayed | - | Real run: tools/conformance.js runs the engine on a golden input. |
| golden-compared | Golden trace compared | matches (boolean) | Real run: tools/conformance.js compares the engine's trace with the golden one and finds the first differing step and field. |
| engine-diverges | Engine output differs | - | Environment: the engine under test returns a trace that differs from a golden one. |
| conformance-reported | Conformance reported | ok (boolean) | Real run: tools/conformance.js prints its result and exits 0 or non-zero. |
| differential-run | Differential run | - | Real run: node tools/differential.js, the native Rust engine and exegra-lean on golden and generated inputs. |
| differential-mismatch-saved | Mismatch saved | - | Real run: tools/differential.js writes the mismatching input and its result under test-results/differential/. |
| sim-load-requested | Scenario loaded in the panel | scenario (string) | Real run: recorded by loadSim of src/app/session.js when a scenario's engine input was derived (from session.simLoad and simSetFaults of src/ui/sim-panel.js, a remembered scenario on open, and a semantic commit while a scenario is loaded). |
| engine-run-intent | Run handed to the runner | token (number), scenario (string) | Real run: recorded by loadSim (src/app/session.js) just before runner.run(input); token is the session's simToken of the run. |
| worker-terminated | Worker terminated | - | A new run while one is waiting stops the worker. Real run: recorded by stop() of src/app/engine-runner.js when a worker exists (worker.terminate(), waiting.clear()). |
| worker-run-done | Worker finished the run | id (number), input (string), generation (number) | Stands for the time the worker spends in runEngineText (src/app/engine-worker.js onmessage). Real run: recorded by worker.onmessage of src/app/engine-runner.js when the reply arrives, just before the reply itself; generation counts the workers the runner terminated. |
| worker-load-failed | Worker could not load | - | Real run: recorded by worker.onerror in src/app/engine-runner.js; the waiting jobs rerun on the main thread from then on. |
| sim-trace-ready | Trace handed to the session | token (number), steps (number) | Real run: recorded when the promise of runner.run resolves in loadSim, before the token check; steps is the length of the trace. The session keeps it only if its token is still current. |
| sim-run-failed | Run failed | token (number) | Real run: recorded in the rejection branch of runner.run in loadSim, before the token check (session.note error). |
| sim-step-requested | Step | - | Real run: recorded by session.simStep (src/app/session.js) from the panel, the transport or the play timer, outside Live mode. |
| sim-back-requested | Step back | - | Real run: recorded by session.simBack outside Live mode. |
| sim-run-to-end-requested | Run to end | - | Real run: recorded by session.simRunToEnd outside Live mode. |
| sim-step-shown | Step shown | stepIndex (number) | Real run: recorded by session.simStep when it revealed or moved to a step, before simChanged(true) emits sim with pulse true. |
| step-pulsed | Step pulsed in the scene | stepIndex (number) | Real run: recorded by the sim listener of src/app/main.js when it pulses the responsible entities of a simulated step and draws its message flow (view.pulse, view.flow); not in Live mode, where the pulses show recorded events. |
| page-loaded | Page loaded | persistent (boolean), project (string) | The application module starts. Real run: the first statement of src/app/main.js records it; persistent is PERSISTENCE_AVAILABLE (import.meta.env.DEV) of src/persist/client.js, project is the project the page asks for (requestedProject of src/app/session.js: ?project=, else the one remembered in localStorage, else exegra). |
| engine-load-requested | Engine load | - | Real run: initEngine() in src/app/main.js (src/core/engine.js loads the .wasm). |
| engine-load-settled | Engine loaded or failed | ok (boolean) | Real run: the then of initEngine() in src/app/main.js (success, or the error noted and the session started anyway). |
| entity-selected | Entity selected | id (string) | Real run: recorded by session.select(id) when the selection changes, from the scene, explorer, search or a link; id is none for a cleared selection. |
| view-selected | View selected | view (string) | Real run: recorded by session.setView(viewId) from the toolbar when the view changes. The switch itself is view-switched; this model reads that one. |
| preferences-written | Session preferences written | selection (string), viewId (string) | Real run: persistUi in src/app/session.js, localStorage.setItem("exegra.session", ...). Recorded only when the selection or the view of the open project differs from the last one written: a write that leaves both unchanged is a stutter step of this model. |
| page-reloaded | Page reloaded | - | The tab is (re)loaded; in-memory state is gone and session.open restores what the store remembers. Real run: recorded at the start of createSession in src/app/session.js, once per page load. |
| preferences-read | Session preferences read | - | Real run: recorded at the start of createSession in src/app/session.js, just before loadStore reads localStorage.getItem("exegra.session"). |
| preferences-loaded | Session preferences loaded | selection (string), viewId (string) | Real run: recorded by session.open (src/app/session.js) after it applied store.perProject[name] (selection, view, collapsed, ...), on every open; selection is the restored selection or none. |
| panel-toggled | Panel opened or closed | open (boolean) | Real run: recorded by persist() of src/ui/layout.js when the explorer's open state it is about to store differs from the last stored one (the responsive layout opened or closed the explorer). |
| layout-preferences-written | Layout preferences written | explorerOpen (boolean) | Real run: persist() in src/ui/layout.js, localStorage.setItem("exegra.layout", ...). Recorded only when explorerOpen changes; a write that leaves it unchanged (a resize, another panel) is a stutter step of this model. |
| camera-moved | Camera moved | camera (string) | Pointer or wheel input on the scene ends. Real run: cameraMoved, the pointerup and wheel listener of src/app/main.js, records it with the camera (JSON of view.getCamera()) and calls saveCamera. |
| view-switched | View switched (camera) | view (string) | The view the scene shows changes. Real run: recorded by recompute() of src/app/session.js just before the scene is projected, once per change: after session.setView, after session.open (the opened project's view), and when the session replaces a view the specification does not declare with the first declared one. pushScene in src/app/main.js then restores the camera stored for the view, and the view listener calls saveCamera. |
| camera-saved | Camera stored | view (string), camera (string) | Real run: saveCamera in src/app/main.js records it just before sessionStorage.setItem("exegra.camera.<project>.<view>", ...); camera is the JSON of view.getCamera(). |
| camera-restore-requested | Stored camera read | view (string) | Real run: recorded by pushScene (src/app/main.js) before sessionStorage.getItem(cameraKey()), once per project and view. |
| camera-restored | Stored camera applied | view (string), camera (string) | Real run: recorded by pushScene after view.setCamera(JSON.parse(saved)); camera is the stored text. |
| camera-none | No stored camera | view (string) | Real run: recorded by pushScene when nothing is stored for the view; the current camera is kept. |
| traffic-step-recorded | Traffic step recorded | event (string) | The traffic recorder appends one event of a real run (src/app/traffic-recorder.js record). Not itself recorded: it stands for the append. |
| sim-reset-requested | Simulation reset requested | - | Real run: recorded by session.simReset (src/app/session.js) outside Live mode; the cursor hides every step again. |
| sim-seek-requested | Simulation seek requested | stepIndex (number) | Real run: recorded by session.simSeek (src/app/session.js) outside Live mode, from a row of the timeline, a request line or a check's jump; stepIndex is the step asked for. |

## Faults

| Id | Name | Kind | Event | Transition | Occurrence | By | At | Description |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |
| validation-fails | Candidate fails validation | enable-transition | - | validator-reject | - | - | - | The operation produces a specification with validation errors, so the validator rejects the candidate. |
| disk-changed-externally | File changed on disk since it was read | enable-transition | - | workspace-detect-conflict | - | - | - | Another process changed spec.jsonc after the session last read or wrote it, so the base hash no longer matches. |
| publish-lost | Model publication lost | drop-event | Model published | - | - | - | - | The session never publishes the refreshed model, so the inspector and the source editor keep showing the old revision. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| refresh-before-commit | Refresh not caused by a commit | inject-event | Refresh requested | - | - | - | 0 | The projection is refreshed from an uncommitted model. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| save-before-refresh | Write not caused by a refresh | inject-event | Save requested | - | - | - | 0 | The file is written before the views were refreshed from the committed model. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| commit-skips-validation | Commit without validation | enable-transition | - | tx-commit-unvalidated | - | - | - | The transaction commits as soon as the candidate is parsed, before the validator has passed it. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| reject-partially-applied | Rejected operation partially applied | enable-transition | - | tx-reject-partially-applied | - | - | - | A rejected operation still advances the revision. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| rejection-refreshes | Rejection treated as a commit | enable-transition | - | session-refresh-on-rejection | - | - | - | The session refreshes, and then saves, after a rejected transaction. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| conflict-overwrites | Write despite a conflict | enable-transition | - | workspace-overwrite-on-conflict | - | - | - | The workspace detects the conflict but writes the file anyway. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| conflict-swallowed | Conflict reported as saved | enable-transition | - | session-swallow-conflict | - | - | - | The session receives the conflict and reports the edit as saved. A defect the edit flow must not have. |
| session-reload-keeps-text | Reload from disk keeps the in-memory text | enable-transition | - | session-resolve-reload-keeps-text | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. resolveConflict('reload') clears the conflict without adopting the disk text. |
| session-overwrite-keeps-stale-base | Overwrite keeps the stale base hash | enable-transition | - | session-overwrite-stale-base | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. resolveConflict('overwrite') writes with the base hash that just conflicted. |
| session-reloads-own-write | Own write reloaded as an external change | enable-transition | - | session-reload-own-write | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. onExternalChange treats the notice of the session's own write as an external change. |
| session-writes-back-external-change | Adopted change written back | enable-transition | - | session-flush-external-reload | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. a commit with origin external is flushed to disk. |
| session-skips-flush-on-undo | Undo not saved | enable-transition | - | session-skip-flush-on-undo | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. a commit with origin undo is not written, so the file keeps the undone text. |
| undo-keeps-text | Undo does not restore the text | enable-transition | - | history-undo-keeps-text | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. Project.undo records a step without restoring the earlier text. |
| move-edits-spec | Layout move edits the specification | enable-transition | - | history-move-edits-spec | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. MovePresentationNode changes the specification text. |
| toolbar-import-skips-syntax-check | Import sends unparseable text | enable-transition | - | toolbar-import-unchecked | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. the import handler sends a bundle whose specification has syntax errors. |
| toolbar-import-skips-reopen | Imported project not reopened | enable-transition | - | toolbar-import-skip-reopen | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. the toolbar does not reopen the project after an import. |
| generate-from-stale-model | Documents generated from an earlier model | enable-transition | - | realisation-generate-stale | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. the Realisation panel writes documents derived from an earlier model. |
| readonly-build-writes | Production build tries to write | enable-transition | - | session-readonly-flush | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. a read-only build flushes a commit. |
| session-started-before-engine | Session started before the engine settled | enable-transition | - | app-init-before-engine | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. main.js starts the session before initEngine has settled. |
| open-ignores-read-hashes | Open drops the hashes of the read | enable-transition | - | session-opened-without-base | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. session.open does not keep the hashes it read as base hashes. |
| journal-append-fails | Journal append fails after the replace | enable-transition | - | ws-write-journal-append-fails | - | - | - | The disk fills (or the journal cannot be written) after atomicWrite replaced the file: the file holds the new text, no journal line is written and the write is answered as a failure. Read from the code (tools/workspace.js write()); not run. |
| change-notice-lost | Change notice lost | drop-event | File changed | - | - | - | - | A change notice never reaches the session: the event stream was down, and a stream reopened afterwards takes its baseline from disk, so the change is not notified again. Read from the code (tools/workspace.js watch()); not run. |
| save-lands-after-export | Save lands after the export | delay-event | Write intended | - | - | 5 | - | The write of a committed edit reaches the server only after an export has read the files. |
| claims-reevaluation-skipped | Re-evaluation after a commit skipped | drop-event | Claims re-evaluated | - | 2 | - | - | A defect the code must not have. the commit does not re-evaluate the claims (the second request of the run, the first being the open's). |
| sim-steps-past-end | Step past the end of the trace | enable-transition | - | sim-step-past-end | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. simStep moves beyond the last recorded step. |
| sim-adopts-stale-trace | Stale trace adopted | enable-transition | - | sim-adopt-stale | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. loadSim adopts a trace without checking its token. |
| runner-keeps-stale-job | Replaced job still answered | enable-transition | - | runner-keep-stale-job | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. a new run neither terminates the worker nor drops the replaced job. |
| runner-forwards-stale-reply | Reply of a replaced job forwarded | enable-transition | - | runner-forward-stale | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. the runner hands the reply of a replaced job to the session. |
| pulse-without-step | Pulse not caused by a step | inject-event | Step pulsed in the scene | - | - | - | 0 | A defect the code must not have. the scene pulses a step the session did not show. |
| preferences-not-stored | Preferences not stored | drop-event | Session preferences written | - | - | - | - | localStorage refuses the write (private window, quota, storage disabled); persistUi catches the error and nothing is remembered. |
| camera-save-lost | Camera not stored | drop-event | Camera stored | - | 1 | - | - | sessionStorage refuses the first camera write; saveCamera catches the error and the camera is not remembered. |
| apply-reports-ok-after-layout-conflict | Apply reports success after a layout conflict | enable-transition | - | cli-apply-ok-after-layout-conflict | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. exegra apply reports ok although the layout write conflicted. |
| cli-failure-omits-partial-write | Failure hides the partial write | enable-transition | - | cli-apply-failure-omits-spec | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. the write.conflict failure does not say that spec.jsonc was already written. |
| verify-reports-before-recording | Verify reports before recording | enable-transition | - | cli-verify-report-early | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. exegra verify prints its envelope before evidence/results.json is written. |
| trace-written-on-refusal | Trace file written on refusal | enable-transition | - | cli-trace-written-on-refusal | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. exegra trace --out writes a file although the engine refused the run. |
| prove-reports-unchecked | Unchecked exploration reported | enable-transition | - | prove-report-unchecked | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. exegra prove reports a claim result that Lean did not accept. |
| markdown-writes-despite-gate | Import written past the coverage gate | enable-transition | - | md-write-despite-gate | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. tools/import-markdown.js writes the specification although its coverage check failed. |
| conformance-ignores-mismatch | Mismatch reported as agreement | enable-transition | - | conformance-ignore-mismatch | - | - | - | A defect the code must not have. tools/conformance.js reports agreement for a differing trace. |

## Scenarios

### A valid visual edit

A valid edit made visually: it is submitted, validated and committed (revision 2), then the projection is refreshed and shown by the renderer, inspector and source editor, and only then the file is written, asynchronously one tick later. Real order: refresh at commit, write afterwards. Ends quiescent with every representation at revision 2 and the file persisted.

- Behaviors: Interaction layer: submit an edit and show its outcome, Source editor: apply a draft and stay in sync, Transaction system: check, validate, commit or reject, Parser: derive the candidate model, Validator: judge the candidate model, Session: refresh on commit, then save asynchronously, Projection: derive the scene of the committed revision, Renderer: draw the refreshed scene, Inspector: refresh from the committed model, File service: replace the file or report a conflict

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Edit requested | - | An inspector property change, a dragged node or a dialog submission. |

### A valid source edit

The same pipeline entered from the source editor as a ReplaceSource operation. The refresh and the asynchronous write follow the commit in the same order as for a visual edit.

- Behaviors: Interaction layer: submit an edit and show its outcome, Source editor: apply a draft and stay in sync, Transaction system: check, validate, commit or reject, Parser: derive the candidate model, Validator: judge the candidate model, Session: refresh on commit, then save asynchronously, Projection: derive the scene of the committed revision, Renderer: draw the refreshed scene, Inspector: refresh from the committed model, File service: replace the file or report a conflict

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Source apply requested | - | The user applies a valid draft. |

### An edit that fails validation

The operation fails validation: the transaction is rejected, nothing commits, nothing refreshes and nothing is written; the interaction layer shows the rejection.

- Behaviors: Interaction layer: submit an edit and show its outcome, Source editor: apply a draft and stay in sync, Transaction system: check, validate, commit or reject, Parser: derive the candidate model, Validator: judge the candidate model, Session: refresh on commit, then save asynchronously, Projection: derive the scene of the committed revision, Renderer: draw the refreshed scene, Inspector: refresh from the committed model, File service: replace the file or report a conflict

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Edit requested | - | An edit whose result would have validation errors. |

#### Active faults

- Candidate fails validation (`validation-fails`)

### A valid edit whose write conflicts with the disk

The edit commits and every view shows revision 2, but the file on disk changed since it was read: the write reports a conflict, the file is not overwritten and the session flags the conflict.

- Behaviors: Interaction layer: submit an edit and show its outcome, Source editor: apply a draft and stay in sync, Transaction system: check, validate, commit or reject, Parser: derive the candidate model, Validator: judge the candidate model, Session: refresh on commit, then save asynchronously, Projection: derive the scene of the committed revision, Renderer: draw the refreshed scene, Inspector: refresh from the committed model, File service: replace the file or report a conflict

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Edit requested | - | A valid edit while the file on disk has changed. |

#### Active faults

- File changed on disk since it was read (`disk-changed-externally`)

### A write with a stale base hash is refused and retried

Contract events between the persistence client and the development server's write endpoint (contract write-file): the first request carries a stale base hash and is refused with a conflict; the client retries with the current hash and the write is accepted. The trace reads as a request log: a request, a refused write and a retry.

- Behaviors: Persistence client: write a file, retry after a conflict, Development server: accept a write or refuse it as a conflict

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Write intended | {"baseHash":"stale","file":"spec","name":"exegra","requestId":1,"text":"9b2f1c0e"} | The session writes the committed text; the client's base hash is out of date. |

### Start the application and open a project

main.js loads the engine, then the session lists the projects, opens exegra by reading its files (GET /__exegra/projects, GET /__exegra/project), takes their hashes as base hashes, evaluates the claims and subscribes to changes (GET /__exegra/events). Pipelines P25 and P10.

- Behaviors: Application start: load the engine, then start the session, Engine module: load the WebAssembly, Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Workspace watcher: notify hash changes of tracked files, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload, Session: re-evaluate claims on open, semantic commits and recorded changes, Claim evaluator: evaluate every claim, synchronously

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Page loaded | {"persistent":true,"project":"exegra"} | Served by the development server. |

### An edit in a production build is never saved

A production build (PERSISTENCE_AVAILABLE false) opens the shipped specification bundled in dist/ (copy shipped-specifications), reports the save state read-only, and an edit commits in memory without any write. Pipeline P26.

- Behaviors: Application start: load the engine, then start the session, Engine module: load the WebAssembly, Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Page loaded | {"persistent":false,"project":"exegra"} | A static build. |
| 3 | Edit applied | {"origin":"ui","specHash":"h2"} | A valid edit. |

### A save conflict resolved by reloading from disk

Another process replaces spec.jsonc while the session commits an edit: the write is refused as a conflict (nothing overwritten, no journal line), the change notice that follows is ignored because the save is in conflict, and the user chooses "Reload from disk": the disk text is adopted with Project.reload, the in-memory edit is dropped and nothing is written. Pipelines P04 and P05.

- Behaviors: Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Workspace watcher: notify hash changes of tracked files, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Project opened by the user | {"name":"exegra"} | The session opens the project and subscribes to its changes. |
| 3 | File replaced outside the session | {"file":"spec","hash":"hx"} | An editor or an agent replaces the file. |
| 3 | Edit applied | {"origin":"ui","specHash":"h2"} | The session commits an edit at the same moment. |
| 6 | Conflict resolution chosen | {"how":"reload"} | The user chooses Reload from disk. |

### A save conflict resolved by overwriting disk

As conflict-resolved-by-reload, but the user chooses "Overwrite disk": the disk hash becomes the base hash and the in-memory text is written over the other process's change. Pipelines P04 and P05.

- Behaviors: Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Workspace watcher: notify hash changes of tracked files, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Project opened by the user | {"name":"exegra"} | The session opens the project and subscribes to its changes. |
| 3 | File replaced outside the session | {"file":"spec","hash":"hx"} | An editor or an agent replaces the file. |
| 3 | Edit applied | {"origin":"ui","specHash":"h2"} | The session commits an edit at the same moment. |
| 6 | Conflict resolution chosen | {"how":"overwrite"} | The user chooses Overwrite disk. |

### A change made outside the session is adopted

With the project open and nothing pending, another process replaces spec.jsonc: the watcher notices the new hash after its debounce, the session re-reads the project and adopts the text with Project.reload (origin external, undo history cleared) and writes nothing. Pipeline P06.

- Behaviors: Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Workspace watcher: notify hash changes of tracked files, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Project opened by the user | {"name":"exegra"} | The session opens the project and subscribes to its changes. |
| 3 | File replaced outside the session | {"file":"spec","hash":"hx"} | git checkout, an editor or an agent. |

### The notice of the session's own write is ignored

An edit is saved; the watcher then notifies the change of spec.jsonc, which is the session's own write: its hash equals the base hash just acknowledged, so nothing is re-read or reloaded. Pipelines P01 and P06.

- Behaviors: Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Workspace watcher: notify hash changes of tracked files, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Project opened by the user | {"name":"exegra"} | The session opens the project and subscribes to its changes. |
| 3 | Edit applied | {"origin":"ui","specHash":"h2"} | A valid edit. |

### Undo restores the earlier text and saves it

An edit is committed and saved, then undone: Project.undo restores the whole earlier text by a commit with origin undo, which the session writes like any edit, so the file holds the original text again and the journal has one line per accepted write. Pipeline P07.

- Behaviors: Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload

| 3 | Edit applied | {"origin":"ui","specHash":"h2"} | A valid edit. |
| 6 | Undo requested | - | Undo from the toolbar or the keyboard. |

### A dragged node is saved to the layout file only

A node dragged in the scene becomes MovePresentationNode: a layout-only commit (no claim re-evaluation, semantic hash unchanged), written to layout.jsonc with its own base hash; spec.jsonc is not written. Pipeline P14.

- Behaviors: Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Project: commits, undo, redo and reload

### A semantic commit re-evaluates the claims

After the open's evaluation, a valid edit commits and onCommit re-evaluates every claim synchronously on the main thread (trace and metric claims run engine scenarios there), so the claim results are stamped with the new model. Pipeline P11.

### Import a project bundle over the open project

The user imports a bundle into the open project's name: the toolbar checks its syntax, POST /__exegra/import overwrites spec.jsonc without a transaction, a base-hash check or a journal line (claim edits-only-via-transactions is violated by this path), the watcher notices the change, and the toolbar refreshes the project list and reopens the project, which then shows the imported text. Pipeline P08.

### A bundle with syntax errors is refused before it is sent

The toolbar's analyzeSpec finds syntax errors in the bundle's spec.jsonc and refuses it; nothing is sent and nothing on disk changes. A bundle with validation errors only would be sent. Pipeline P08.

### Generate the documents from the Realisation panel

After an edit is saved, the Realisation panel derives the five documents from the session's current model and writes them with POST /__exegra/generated; the workspace rewrites the changed files under generated/, with no journal line and no change notice. Pipeline P23.

The traffic recorder counts the request and the answer of one write with their requestId (traffic-recorder-flow counts them; the real recorder appends them to its stream, src/app/traffic-recorder.js). Pipeline P28. Live mode replays the stream itself through live-session.

### Run a scenario in the Simulation panel and step through it

The panel loads a scenario: the engine input is posted to the worker, the trace comes back by id and becomes the cursor; the user steps four times through a three-step trace (the fourth press changes nothing), steps back and runs to the end. Each step taken forward is pulsed in the scene. Pipelines P12 and P13.

- Behaviors: Session: load a scenario, adopt its trace, step through it, Engine runner: one job at a time in the worker, Engine worker: run an input, reply with its trace, Scene: pulse the step just taken

#### Steps

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Scenario loaded in the panel | {"scenario":"visual-edit-flow"} | Load a scenario. |
| 4 | Step | - | - |
| 5 | Step | - | - |
| 6 | Step | - | - |
| 7 | Step | - | One press more than the trace has steps. |
| 8 | Step back | - | - |
| 9 | Run to end | - | - |

### A second load replaces a run in progress

A scenario is loaded and, before its trace arrives, another is loaded: the runner terminates the worker, so the first run is never answered, and the session would discard it anyway because its token is stale. Only the second trace is adopted. Pipeline P12.

- Behaviors: Session: load a scenario, adopt its trace, step through it, Engine runner: one job at a time in the worker, Engine worker: run an input, reply with its trace

| At | Event | Payload | Note |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Scenario loaded in the panel | {"scenario":"visual-edit-flow"} | First load. |
| 1 | Scenario loaded in the panel | {"scenario":"rejected-edit"} | Second load while the first is running. |

### Interaction state survives a reload

The user selects an entity and switches to the Data view, opens a panel, then reloads the page: the session restores the selection and the view from localStorage exegra.session (and the responsive layout its arrangement from exegra.layout). Pipeline P16.

| 2 | Panel opened or closed | {"open":false} | - |
| 3 | Page reloaded | - | - |

The user moves the camera in the structural view, switches to the Data view (which has no stored camera, so it keeps and stores the structural camera), moves the camera there and switches back: the structural camera is restored from sessionStorage. Pipeline P17.

### exegra apply writes the specification and the layout

An agent applies operations that change both files: the command reads the project, applies the transaction in process, writes spec.jsonc and then layout.jsonc with the hashes it read (two journal lines) and prints a success envelope. Pipelines P18 and P19.

- Behaviors: Agent command line: one command per process, Workspace service: the project files on disk

| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| 0 | Command invoked | {"command":"apply","options":"--file ops.json","requestId":1} | node cli/exegra.js apply --file ops.json |

### exegra apply after the layout changed on disk: a partial write

As cli-apply, but another process replaces layout.jsonc after the command read it: spec.jsonc is written (with its journal line), the layout write conflicts, and the command fails with write.conflict saying that the specification was already written. The files now hold the new specification and the other process's layout: a change that bypasses the transaction's atomicity (finding F05). Pipeline P19.

- Behaviors: Agent command line: one command per process, Workspace service: the project files on disk

| At | Event | Payload | Note |

| 1 | File replaced outside the session | {"file":"layout","hash":"Lx"} | The application saves a dragged node meanwhile. |

### exegra verify while the application has the project open

The application has the project open and watches it; an agent runs exegra verify, which reads the project, runs the test evidence, replaces evidence/results.json (no journal line) and re-evaluates the claims before printing its envelope. The watcher notices the recorded results; the session re-reads them and re-evaluates its claims. Pipelines P20 and P11.

- Behaviors: Agent command line: one command per process, Test runner: execute the test evidence, Workspace service: the project files on disk, Workspace watcher: notify hash changes of tracked files, Development server: route each endpoint to the workspace service, Persistence client: one HTTP call per request, Session: open, save, conflicts and external changes, Session: re-evaluate claims on open, semantic commits and recorded changes, Claim evaluator: evaluate every claim, synchronously

| 3 | Command invoked | {"command":"verify","options":"--skip-browser","requestId":1} | node cli/exegra.js verify |

### Live: this running Exegra, replayed

The behaviors that the simulator panel's Live mode replays the running application's own recorded events through (src/core/replay.js, component self-model-replay). It has no input steps: its input is the stream of the traffic recorder, from page load on. Run as an ordinary scenario it does nothing.

## Claims

| Id | Kind | Status | Statement |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| core-independent-of-ui | structural | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | No component of the semantic core has a dependency relationship (depends-on, calls, subscribes, reads, writes or submits-edits), directly or through any chain of them, to the application, the visualization, the persistence subsystem, the agent interface, the tooling, the DOM and browser platform, browser storage, Three.js, Vite, the file system or the test runner. |
| core-dependencies-acyclic | structural | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | The dependency relationships among the components of the semantic core form no cycle. |
| edits-only-via-transactions | structural | Violated | Following relationships that carry edits and file content (submits-edits, data-flow, writes), every path from the editing surfaces (inspector, source editor, dialogs, visual interaction, toolbar, shell) to the specification file passes through the transaction system. |
| editing-surfaces-except-import-via-transactions | structural | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | Following submits-edits, data-flow and writes relationships, every path from the inspector, source editor, dialogs, visual interaction and shell to the specification file passes through the transaction system. |
| renderer-does-not-own-state | structural | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | The Three.js renderer has no writes or submits-edits relationship to the specification file, the layout file, the transaction system or the session. |
| storage-has-owner | structural | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | Every data store (meta-model, claim method registry and the project files) declares an owner. |
| agent-interface-uses-transactions | structural | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | The agent command line reaches the transaction system along its relationships. |
| transactions-provide-apply-contract | structural | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | The transaction system provides a port with the apply-transaction contract. |
| edit-representations-equivalent | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | After a valid edit, at quiescence the scene (projection and renderer), the inspector and the source editor all show the revision of the transaction system, for a visual edit and for a source edit. |
| file-matches-model-after-save | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | After a valid edit, at quiescence the file on disk holds the committed revision and the session reports saved, for a visual edit and for a source edit. |
| refresh-follows-commit | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | Every projection refresh is preceded by a delivered transaction commit: views are refreshed from a committed model, never from a candidate. |
| save-follows-refresh | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | Every write request to the file service is preceded by a projection refresh: the write is asynchronous and happens after the views were refreshed, in all three edits that commit. |
| commit-only-after-validation | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | Every commit of an operation transaction (the edit flows modelled here) is preceded by a delivered validation-passed: no operation transaction commits without a validation with no errors. |
| rejected-edit-changes-nothing | invariant | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | In the rejected edit, at every step the revision of the transaction system, of the projection, renderer, inspector and source editor and of the file on disk is still the initial revision, and the session still reports saved. |
| rejected-edit-never-refreshes | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | In the rejected edit the projection is never refreshed. |
| rejected-edit-never-persists | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | In the rejected edit no write to the file service is ever requested. |
| conflict-never-overwrites | invariant | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | In the edit whose write conflicts with the disk, at every step, once the conflict is flagged the revision persisted on disk is still the initial one: the conflicting file is never overwritten. |
| conflict-is-flagged | temporal | Verified within the stated model and assumptions | In the edit whose write conflicts with the disk, at quiescence the session reports a conflict, every view shows the new revision and the file on disk is behind the model. |
| layout-moves-are-not-semantic | invariant | Supported by external evidence | Moving a node changes only the layout file: the semantic hash and the specification text cannot change. |
| simulation-deterministic | invariant | Supported by external evidence | Running a scenario twice with the same model and faults gives deep-equal traces, and stepping one event at a time gives the same trace as running to the end. |
| generation-deterministic | invariant | Supported by external evidence | Two runs of the generators over the same model and inputs produce byte-identical artifacts. |
| persistence-confined-to-workspace | invariant | Supported by external evidence | Project names are validated and every path is resolved inside specs/<project>/ before any read or write; names and paths that escape that directory are refused, and generated files can only be written under generated/. The one exception is deliberate: a project.jsonc may name a specification file outside the project directory (a linked project, such as a specification kept in its own repository), which is then read and written there. project.jsonc is not writable through the workspace endpoints (write and import accept only spec.jsonc, layout.jsonc and evidence/results.json), so only a person editing the file on disk can point a project elsewhere. |
| invalid-source-keeps-last-valid-model | invariant | Supported by external evidence | An invalid source draft is rejected with diagnostics, the committed model is not changed and the draft stays as typed in the source editor. |
| unsupported-constructs-are-diagnosed | invariant | Supported by external evidence | A specification construct the meta-model does not describe (an unknown section, entity property, vocabulary field or claim parameter) is reported as a diagnostic and never silently dropped. |
| benchmark-scene-interactive | measured | Not evaluated | The default structural view of specs/benchmark/spec.jsonc (396 components and 507 relationships) renders at an interactive frame rate on ordinary desktop hardware, measured as the fps reported by the scene view's getStats() while the camera orbits. The threshold for 'interactive' is not defined in this specification. |
